# Nightrun: every asset still pending (2026-10-21)

Generated by `games-src/scripts/pending-assets.py` at 2026-10-21 17:37Z. Re-run it after any asset lands; `games-src/nightrun/pending-test.js` checks that every picture id a code hook asks for is listed here. No art was created for this report.

**Status words.** `not delivered` = nobody has the picture: paint it. `delivered-unwired` = the owner already generated it in Flow (viewer stage generated / approved / pushed) but it is not in `media/` or `ready.json`: ingest and wire, do not repaint. `superseded?` = an approved animated sheet probably covers the job: **needs owner confirmation** before anyone paints. The hatched placeholder files in `media/` are not art and do not count as delivered.

Rules are the `PRI_RULES` list in the script.

## Pending from workers

- `ASSETS-NEEDED-STREET.md`: pending from worker (Wxx, file not on origin at run time; re-run this script after it lands)
- `ASSETS-NEEDED-ENEMIES.md`: pending from worker (Wxx, file not on origin at run time; re-run this script after it lands)
- `ASSETS-NEEDED-FX.md`: pending from worker (Wxx, file not on origin at run time; re-run this script after it lands)
- `ASSETS-NEEDED-STORY.md`: pending from worker (Wxx, file not on origin at run time; re-run this script after it lands)
- `ASSETS-NEEDED-COVERAGE.md`: pending from worker (Wxx, file not on origin at run time; re-run this script after it lands)
- `ASSET-DECISIONS.md`: pending from worker (Wxx, file not on origin at run time; re-run this script after it lands)

When a worker file lands, run `python3 games-src/scripts/pending-assets.py` again: its table rows are ingested under the same columns (id, size, pri, where, what to paint / prompt).
